### The Alkali, &c., Works Regulation Act 1906

*Extent: E+W+S*

**1.** (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-c64b0707828f220669b7b7b4d08eb06f] The *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c64b0707828f220669b7b7b4d08eb06f] [^c14548351]Alkali, &c, Works Regulation Act 1906 shall be amended in accordance with the following provisions of this paragraph. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-c64b0707828f220669b7b7b4d08eb06f]

### The Statistics of Trade Act 1947

*Extent: E+W+S*

**2.** In the [^c14548361]Statistics of Trade Act 1947, after section 9 (restrictions on disclosure of information) there shall be inserted—

> **9A. Exceptions from section 9.**
>
> (1) Nothing in section nine of this Act shall prevent or penalise the disclosure by the Secretary of State of information obtained under this Act—
>
> - (a) to the Environment Agency or the Scottish Environment Protection Agency; or
> - (b) to an officer of either of those Agencies authorised by that Agency to receive the information.
>
> (2) A person to whom information is disclosed in pursuance of the last foregoing subsection shall not use the information for any purpose other than the purposes of any functions of the Agency in question.

### The Rivers (Prevention of Pollution) (Scotland) Act 1951

*Extent: E+W+S*

**3.** (1) The [^c14548371]Rivers (Prevention of Pollution) (Scotland) Act 1951 shall be amended in accordance with the following provisions of this paragraph.

(2)[^key-17a2bf5f8c23faa8d74aa81be6c7c9bb] . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

(3) In section 18 (provision and obtaining of information)—

- (a) in subsection (1) (power to obtain information)—
  - (i) for the word “them” in each place where it occurs there shall be substituted the word “ it ”;
  - (ii) for the words “a river purification authority” there shall be substituted the words “ SEPA ”; and
  - (iii) the words “of their area”, “in their area” (where first occurring) and “in their area or any part thereof” shall cease to have effect;
- (b) in subsection (2) (Secretary of State’s power to give directions) for the words “any river purification authority” and “the authority” there shall be substituted the words “ SEPA ”, and for the word “them” there shall be substituted the word “ it ”; and
- (c) in subsection (3) (duty to provide reasonable facilities for inspection of records)—
  - (i) for the words “Every river purification authority” and “the river purification authority” there shall be substituted the words “ SEPA ”;
  - (ii) for the word “them” there shall be substituted the word “ it ”; and
  - (iii) the words “in their area” and the words from “whose” to “**authority**” where it next occurs shall cease to have effect; and
- (d) in subsection (6) (interpretation of “stream”) for the words “the river purification authority’s” there shall be substituted the words “ SEPA’s ”.

(4) In section 19 (power to take samples of effluents)—

- (a) in subsection (1) (power to obtain and take away samples of water from any stream or effluent)—
  - (i) for the words “A river purification authority” there shall be substituted the words “ SEPA ”; and
  - (ii) the words “in the area of the authority” shall cease to have effect; and
- (b) in subsection (3) (interpretation of “stream”) for the words “the river purification authority’s” there shall be substituted the words “ SEPA’s ”.

(5) In section 35 (interpretation)—

- (a) the definitions of “river purification authority”, “river purification board” and “river purification board area” shall cease to have effect; and
- (b) there shall be inserted at the appropriate place—

  > “**SEPA**” means the Scottish Environment Protection Agency;

  .

### The Opencast Coal Act 1958

**13.** The [^c14548491]Local Authorities (Goods and Services) Act 1970 (supply of goods and services by local authorities to public bodies) shall have effect as if the Agency and SEPA were each both a local authority and a public body for the purposes of that Act other than section 2(2) (accounting requirements in relation to local authority agreements entered into in pursuance of section 1).

### The Agriculture Act 1970

*Extent: E+W+S*

**14.** (1) The [^c14548501]Agriculture Act 1970 shall be amended in accordance with the following provisions of this paragraph.

(2) In section 92(1) (provision of flood warning systems)—

- (a) for the words from the beginning to “**may**” where it first occurs there shall be substituted the words “ The Scottish Environment Protection Agency may ”;
- (b) the words “for their area” and “both within (and in the case of a river purification board) outwith, that area,” shall cease to have effect;
- (c) in sub-paragraph (i) of the proviso—
  - (i) for the words “a river purification board” there shall be substituted the words “ the Scottish Environment Protection Agency ”;
  - (ii) for the word “them” there shall be substituted the word “ it ”; and
  - (iii) for the words “that board” there shall be substituted the words “ the Agency ”; and
- (d) in sub-paragraph (ia) of the proviso for the words following “exercise” to “shall” there shall be substituted the words “ , the Agency shall ”.

(3) In section 92(2)—

- (a) in paragraph (a)(iii) for the words “the authority providing the system” there shall be substituted the words “ the Scottish Environment Protection Agency ”;
- (b) paragraph (c) (definition of “river purification board”) shall cease to have effect.

(4) In section 94 (co-operation with other persons as regards flood warning systems)—

- (a) in subsection (1) for the words following “warning system” to “**may**” where it first occurs there shall be substituted the words “ the Scottish Environment Protection Agency may ” and for the words following “belonging to the” to “for” there shall be substituted the words “ Agency for ”;
- (b) in subsection (2) for the words from the beginning to “may” and for the words following “apparatus of” there shall be substituted the words “ The Agency may ” and “ the Agency ” respectively.

(5) In section 98 (extent of Part VI)—

- (a) for the words from the beginning to “England” there shall be substituted the words “ The Scottish Environment Protection Agency ”;
- (b) for the words “section 92(1)(b)” there shall be substituted the words “ section 92(1) ”; and
- (c) for the words “the National Rivers Authority” there shall be substituted the words “ the Environment Agency ”.

### The Prevention of Oil Pollution Act 1971

*Extent: E+W+S*

**15.** (1) The [^c14548511]Prevention of Oil Pollution Act 1971 shall be amended in accordance with the following provisions of this paragraph.

(2) After section 11 (duty to report discharge of oil into waters of harbours) there shall be inserted—

> **11A. Certain provisions not to apply where a discharge or escape is authorised under Part I of the Environmental Protection Act 1990.**
>
> (1) The provisions of sections 2(1) and (2A), 3(1) and 11(1) of this Act shall not apply to any discharge which is made under, and the provisions of section 11(1) of this Act shall not apply to any escape which is authorised by, an authorisation granted under Part I of the [^c14548521]Environmental Protection Act 1990.
>
> (2) This section does not extend to Northern Ireland.

(3) In section 25(1) (power to extend certain provisions of the Act to the Isle of Man etc.), after the words “other than section 3” there shall be inserted the word “ , 11A ”.

  > - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] the entry into controlled waters of any poisonous, noxious or polluting matter or any solid waste matter, or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  > - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] harm to human health, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  >
  > that that subsection should not apply. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  >
  > (5C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] Where the reference falls within subsection (5B) above, if, on the application of the holder or former holder of the consent, the Secretary of State (or other person determining the question referred) determines that *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] acted unreasonably in excluding the application of subsection (5A) above, then— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  >
  > - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] if the reference is still pending at the end of the day on which that determination is made, subsection (5A) above shall apply to the reference from the end of that day; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  > - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] the holder or former holder of the consent shall be entitled to recover compensation from *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] in respect of any loss suffered by him in consequence of the exclusion of the application of that subsection; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  >
  > and any dispute as to a person’s entitlement to such compensation or as to the amount of it shall be determined by a single arbiter appointed, in default of agreement between the parties concerned, by the Secretary of State on the application of any of the parties. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]

  ; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] at the end there shall be added— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]

  > (7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58] This section is subject to section 114 of the Environment Act 1995 (delegation or reference of appeals). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5aa898aa5bcafe4bf19af6fb9eff9d58]
  >

> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may give *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] directions requiring the removal from any register maintained by it under this section of any specified information which is not prescribed for inclusion under subsection (1) of this section or which, by virtue of section 42A or 42B of this Act, ought to have been excluded from the registers. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

(20) For section 42, there shall be substituted the following sections— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

> **42A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Exclusion from registers of information affecting national security. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] No information shall be included in a register kept or maintained by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] under section 41 of this Act if and so long as, in the opinion of the Secretary of State, the inclusion in such a register of that information, or information of that description, would be contrary to the interests of national security. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may, for the purposes of securing the exclusion from registers of information to which subsection (1) of this section applies, give *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] directions— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] specifying information, or descriptions of information, to be excluded from their registers; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] specifying descriptions of information to be referred to the Secretary of State for his determination; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> and no information to be referred to the Secretary of State in pursuance of paragraph (b) of this subsection shall be included in any such register until the Secretary of State determines that it should be so included. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall notify the Secretary of State of any information it excludes from a register in pursuance of directions under subsection (2) of this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A person may, as respects any information which appears to him to be information to which subsection (1) of this section may apply, give a notice to the Secretary of State specifying the information and indicating its apparent nature; and, if he does so— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] he shall notify *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] that he has done so; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] no information so notified to the Secretary of State shall be included in any such register until the Secretary of State has determined that it should be so included. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> **42B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Exclusion from registers of certain confidential information. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] No information relating to the affairs of any individual or business shall, without the consent of that individual or the person for the time being carrying on that business, be included in a register kept or maintained by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] under section 41 of this Act, if and so long as the information— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] is, in relation to him, commercially confidential; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] is not required to be included in the register in pursuance of directions under subsection (7) of this section; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> but information is not commercially confidential for the purposes of this section unless it is determined under this section to be so by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09], or, on appeal, by the Secretary of State. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Where information is furnished to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] for the purpose of— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] an application for a consent under section 34 of this Act; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] complying with any condition of such a consent; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] complying with a notice under section 93 of this Act, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> then, if the person furnishing it applies to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] to have the information excluded from any register kept or maintained by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] under section 41 of this Act, on the ground that it is commercially confidential (as regards himself or another person), *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall determine whether the information is or is not commercially confidential. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A determination under subsection (2) of this section must be made within the period of fourteen days beginning with the date of the application and if *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] fails to make a determination within that period it shall be treated as having determined that the information is commercially confidential. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Where it appears to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] that any information (other than information furnished in circumstances within subsection (2) of this section) which has been obtained by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] under or by virtue of any provision of any enactment might be commercially confidential, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] give to the person to whom or whose business it relates notice that that information is required to be included in a register kept or maintained by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] under section 41 of this Act, unless excluded under this section; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] give him a reasonable opportunity— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] of objecting to the inclusion of the information on the ground that it is commercially confidential; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] of making representations to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] for the purpose of justifying any such objection; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> and, if any representations are made, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall, having taken the representations into account, determine whether the information is or is not commercially confidential. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Where, under subsection (2) or (4) of this section, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] determines that information is not commercially confidential— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the information shall not be entered on the register until the end of the period of twenty-one days beginning with the date on which the determination is notified to the person concerned; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] that person may appeal to the Secretary of State against the decision; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> and, where an appeal is brought in respect of any information, the information shall not be entered on the register pending the final determination or withdrawal of the appeal. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subsections (2), (4) and (7) of section 49B of this Act shall apply in relation to appeals under subsection (5) of this section; but *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] subsection (4) of that section shall have effect for the purposes of this subsection with the substitution for the words from (“which may” onwards of the words “(which must be held in private)”; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] subsection (5) of this section is subject to section 114 of the Environment Act 1995 (delegation or reference of appeals *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] etc *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may give *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] directions as to specified information, or descriptions of information, which the public interest requires to be included in registers kept or maintained by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] under section 41 of this Act notwithstanding that the information may be commercially confidential. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Information excluded from a register shall be treated as ceasing to be commercially confidential for the purposes of this section at the expiry of the period of four years beginning with the date of the determination by virtue of which it was excluded; but the person who furnished it may apply to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] for the information to remain excluded from the register on the ground that it is still commercially confidential and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall determine whether or not that is the case. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subsections (5) and (6) of this section shall apply in relation to a determination under subsection (8) of this section as they apply in relation to a determination under subsection (2) or (4) of this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (10 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may prescribe the substitution (whether in all cases or in such classes or descriptions of case as may be prescribed) for the period for the time being specified in subsection (3) above of such other period as he considers appropriate. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (11 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Information is, for the purposes of any determination under this section, commercially confidential, in relation to any individual or person, if its being contained in register would prejudice to an unreasonable degree the commercial interests of that individual or person. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

(21) In section 46 (operations to remedy or forestall pollution of water)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] in subsection (1)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0e2a99dd7b845e158bc26d0a48d8dd2b][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] at the beginning there shall be inserted the words *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] “ Subject to subsection (1B) below, ” *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the words “in its area” where they first occur and “in its area or elsewhere” shall cease to have effect; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0e2a99dd7b845e158bc26d0a48d8dd2b][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] after subsection (1) there shall be inserted— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

  > (1A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] In either case mentioned in subsection (1) of this section, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall be entitled to carry out investigations for the purpose of establishing the source of the matter and the identity of the person who has caused or knowingly permitted it to be present in controlled waters or at a place from which it was likely, in the opinion of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09], to enter controlled waters. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  >
  > (1B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Without prejudice to the power of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] to carry out investigations under subsection (1A) above, the power conferred by subsection (1) above to carry out operations shall be exercisable only in a case where— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  >
  > - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] considers it necessary to carry out forthwith any operations falling within paragraph (a) or (b) of subsection (1) above; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  > - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] it appears to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09], after reasonable inquiry, that no person can be found on whom to serve a works notice under section 46A of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

  ; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0e2a99dd7b845e158bc26d0a48d8dd2b][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] in subsection (2) after the words “any operations” there shall be inserted the words *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] “ or investigations ” *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
- (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0e2a99dd7b845e158bc26d0a48d8dd2b][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] in subsection (3)(b)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] after the words “any operations” there shall be inserted the words *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] “ or investigations ” *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] after the words “an abandoned mine” there shall be inserted the words *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] “ or an abandoned part of a mine ” *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
- (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-0e2a99dd7b845e158bc26d0a48d8dd2b][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] after subsection (3) there shall be inserted— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

  > (3A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subsection (3)(b) of this section shall not apply to the owner or former operator of any mine or part of a mine if the mine or part in question became abandoned after 31st December 1999. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
  >
  > (3B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subsections (5) and (6) of section 30J above shall apply in relation to subsections (3) and (3A) above as they apply in relation to subsections (3) and (4) of that section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

  . *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

(22)[^key-16c9dfec05b9ea0a07f5cef89af193b9] After section 46 there shall be inserted the following sections— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

> **46A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Notices requiring persons to carry out anti-pollution operations. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subject to the following provisions of this section, where it appears to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] that any poisonous, noxious or polluting matter or any solid waste matter is likely to enter, or to be or to have been present in, any controlled waters, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall be entitled to serve a works notice on any person who, as the case may be,— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] caused or knowingly permitted the matter in question to be present at the place from which it is likely, in the opinion of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09], to enter any controlled waters; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] caused or knowingly permitted the matter in question to be present in any controlled waters. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] For the purposes of this section, a “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**works notice *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**” is a notice requiring the person on whom it is served to carry out such of the following operations as may be specified in the notice, that is to say— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] in a case where the matter in question appears likely to enter any controlled waters, operations for the purpose of preventing it from doing so; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] in a case where the matter appears to be or to have been present in any controlled waters, operations for the purpose— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] of removing or disposing of the matter; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] of remedying or mitigating any pollution caused by its presence in the waters; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] so far as it is reasonably practicable to do so, of restoring the waters, including any flora and fauna dependent on the aquatic environment of the waters, to their state immediately before the matter became present in the waters. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A works notice— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] must specify the periods within which the person on whom it is served is required to do each of the things specified in the notice; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] is without prejudice to the powers of *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] by virtue of section 46(1B)(a) of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Before serving a works notice on any person, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall reasonably endeavour to consult that person concerning the operations which are to be specified in the notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may by regulations make provision for or in connection with— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the form or content of works notices; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] requirements for consultation, before the service of a works notice, with persons other than the person on whom that notice is to be served; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] steps to be taken for the purposes of any consultation required under subsection (4) above or regulations made by virtue of paragraph (b) above; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] any other steps of a procedural nature which are to be taken in connection with, or in consequence of, the service of a works notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A works notice shall not be regarded as invalid, or as invalidly served, by reason only of any failure to comply with the requirements of subsection (4) above or of regulations made by virtue of paragraph (b) of subsection (5) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Nothing in subsection (1) above shall entitle *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] to require the carrying out of any operations which would impede or prevent the making of any discharge in pursuance of a consent given by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] by virtue of section 34 of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (8 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] No works notice shall be served on any person requiring him to carry out any operations in respect of water from an abandoned mine or an abandoned part of a mine which that person permitted to reach such a place as is mentioned in subsection (1)(a) above or to enter any controlled waters. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (9 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subsection (8) above shall not apply to the owner or former operator of any mine or part of a mine if the mine or part in question became abandoned after 31st December 1999. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (10 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Subsections (5) and (6) of section 30J of this Act shall apply in relation to subsections (8) and (9) above as they apply in relation to subsections (3) and (4) of that section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (11 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Where *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] carries out any such investigations as are mentioned in section 46(1A) of this Act, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] serves a works notice on a person in connection with the matter to which the investigations relate, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> it shall (unless the notice is quashed or withdrawn) be entitled to recover the costs or expenses reasonably incurred in carrying out those investigations from that person. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (12 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may, if he thinks fit in relation to any person, give directions to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] as to whether or how it should exercise its powers under this section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> **46B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Grant of, and compensation for, rights of entry (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note) etc. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A works notice may require a person to carry out operations in relation to any land or waters notwithstanding that he is not entitled to carry out those operations. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Any person whose consent is required before any operations required by a works notice may be carried out shall grant, or join in granting, such rights in relation to any land or waters as will enable the person on whom the works notice is served to comply with any requirements imposed by the works notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Before serving a works notice, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall reasonably endeavour to consult every person who appears to it— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] to be the owner or occupier of any relevant land, and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] to be a person who might be required by subsection (2) above to grant, or join in granting, any rights, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> concerning the rights which that person may be so required to grant. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A works notice shall not be regarded as invalid, or as invalidly served, by reason only of any failure to comply with the requirements of subsection (3) above. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A person who grants, or joins in granting, any rights pursuant to subsection (2) above shall be entitled, on making an application within such period as may be prescribed and in such manner as may be prescribed to such person as may be prescribed, to be paid by the person on whom the works notice in question is served compensation of such amount as may be determined in such manner as may be prescribed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Without prejudice to the generality of the regulations that may be made by virtue of subsection (5) above, regulations by virtue of that subsection may make such provision in relation to compensation under this section as may be made by regulations by virtue of subsection (4) of section 35A of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] [^c14548771]Environmental Protection Act 1990 in relation to compensation under that section. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] In this section— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**relevant land *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (a) any land or waters in relation to which the works notice in question requires, or may require, operations to be carried out; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (b) any land adjoining or adjacent to that land or those waters; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**works notice *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**” means a works notice under section 46A of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> **46C (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Appeals against works notices. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A person on whom a works notice is served may, within the period of twenty-one days beginning with the day on which the notice is served, appeal against the notice to the Secretary of State. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] On any appeal under this section the Secretary of State— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] shall quash the notice, if he is satisfied that there is a material defect in the notice; but *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] subject to that, may confirm the notice, with or without modification, or quash it. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] The Secretary of State may by regulations make provision with respect to— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the grounds on which appeals under this section may be made; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the procedure on any such appeal. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] Regulations under subsection (3) above may (among other things)— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] include provisions comparable to those in section 290 of the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] [^c14548781]Public Health Act 1936 (appeals against notices requiring the execution of works); *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] prescribe the cases in which a works notice is, or is not, to be suspended until the appeal is decided, or until some other stage in the proceedings; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] prescribe the cases in which the decision on an appeal may in some respects be less favourable to the appellant than the works notice against which he is appealing; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] prescribe the cases in which the appellant may claim that a works notice should have been served on some other person and prescribe the procedure to be followed in those cases; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (e (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] make provision as respects— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (i (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the particulars to be included in the notice of appeal; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (ii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the persons on whom notice of appeal is to be served and the particulars, if any, which are to accompany the notice; or *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>   - (iii (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] the abandonment of an appeal. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] In this section “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**works notice *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**” means a works notice under section 46A of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] This section is subject to section 114 of the Environment Act 1995 (delegation or reference of appeals). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> **46D (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Consequences of not complying with a works notice. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09][^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] If a person on whom *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] serves a works notice fails to comply with any of the requirements of the notice, he shall be guilty of an offence. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] A person who commits an offence under subsection (1) above shall be liable— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] on summary conviction, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding three months or to a fine not exceeding £20,000 or to both; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] on conviction on indictment, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding two years or to a fine or to both. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] If a person on whom a works notice has been served fails to comply with any of the requirements of the notice, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] may do what that person was required to do and may recover from him any costs or expenses reasonably incurred by *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] in doing it. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] If *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] is of the opinion that proceedings for an offence under subsection (1) above would afford an ineffectual remedy against a person who has failed to comply with the requirements of a works notice, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] may take proceedings in any court of competent jurisdiction for the purpose of securing compliance with the notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09] In this section “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**works notice *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]**” means a works notice under section 46A of this Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-31e67c1282a1986497e2a23e1fb3bd09]

(25) In section 49 (deposit and vegetation in rivers *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e8ee56d6b1d99f0ba90fcfa6d23967eb] etc *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e8ee56d6b1d99f0ba90fcfa6d23967eb]) at the end there shall be added— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e8ee56d6b1d99f0ba90fcfa6d23967eb]

> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-e8ee56d6b1d99f0ba90fcfa6d23967eb] This section is subject to section 114 of the Environment Act 1995 (delegation or reference of appeals). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e8ee56d6b1d99f0ba90fcfa6d23967eb]

> **49A (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Enforcement notices as respects discharge consents. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de][^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] If *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] is of the opinion that the holder of a relevant consent is contravening any condition of the consent, or is likely to contravene any such condition, it may serve on him a notice (an “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**enforcement notice *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**”). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] An enforcement notice shall— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] state that *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] is of the said opinion; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] specify the matters constituting the contravention or the matters making it likely that the contravention will arise; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
> - (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] specify the steps that must be taken to remedy the contravention or, as the case may be, to remedy the matters making it likely that the contravention will arise; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
> - (d (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] specify the period within which those steps must be taken. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] Any person who fails to comply with any requirement imposed by an enforcement notice shall be guilty of an offence and liable— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> - (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] on summary conviction, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding three months or to a fine not exceeding £20,000 or to both; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
> - (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] on conviction on indictment, to imprisonment for a term not exceeding two years or to a fine or to both. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] If *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] is of the opinion that proceedings for an offence under subsection (3) above would afford an ineffectual remedy against a person who has failed to comply with the requirements of an enforcement notice, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] may take proceedings in any court of competent jurisdiction for the purpose of securing compliance with the notice. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] The Secretary of State may, if he thinks fit in relation to any person, give to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] directions as to whether it should exercise its powers under this section and as to the steps which must be taken. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] In this section— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> - “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**relevant consent *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**” means a consent for the purposes of section 30J(7)(a), 34 or 49(1) of this Act; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
> - “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**the holder *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**”, in relation to a relevant consent, is the person who has the consent in question. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> **49B (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note). Appeals against enforcement notices. (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note)[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de][^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]**
>
> (1 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] A person upon whom an enforcement notice has been served under section 49A of this Act may appeal to the Secretary of State. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (2 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] This section is subject to section 114 of the Environment Act 1995 (delegation or reference of appeals *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] etc. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (3 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] An appeal under this section shall, if and to the extent a requirement to do so is prescribed, be advertised in the manner prescribed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (4 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] If either party to the appeal so requests or the Secretary of State so decides, an appeal shall be or continue in the form of a hearing (which may, if the person hearing the appeal so decides, be held, or held to any extent, in private). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (5 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] On the determination of an appeal under this section, the Secretary of State may either quash or affirm the enforcement notice and, if he affirms it, may do so either in its original form or with such modifications as he may in the circumstances think fit. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (6 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] The bringing of an appeal under this section shall not have the effect of suspending the operation of the notice appealed against. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]
>
> (7 (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de] The period within which and the manner in which appeals under this section are to be brought and the manner in which they are to be considered shall be as prescribed. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d6c50a24c10e62a90763a337077474de]

### The Health and Safety at Work etc. Act 1974

*Extent: E+W+S*

**30.** (1) The [^c14548801]Health and Safety at Work etc. Act 1974 (in this paragraph referred to as “**the 1974 Act**”) shall have effect in accordance with the following provisions of this paragraph.

(2) The appropriate new Agency shall, in consequence of the transfer effected by virtue of section 2(2)(c) or, as the case may be, 21(2)(a) of this Act, be regarded for the purposes of Part I of the 1974 Act as the authority which is, by any of the relevant statutory provisions, made responsible in relation to England and Wales or, as the case may be, Scotland for the enforcement of the relevant enactments (and, accordingly, as the enforcing authority in relation to those enactments). *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-1bff0a169172b517753ea4d2fac16c2d]

(3) Neither the Agency nor *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-a3bb0ce3e1fd8414b510ad6b93c961a5] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-a3bb0ce3e1fd8414b510ad6b93c961a5] shall have power to appoint inspectors under section 19 of the 1974 Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-a3bb0ce3e1fd8414b510ad6b93c961a5]

(4) Sections 21 to 23 (improvement notices and prohibition notices) shall have effect in any case where the relevant statutory provision in question is any of the relevant enactments as if references in those sections to an inspector were references to the appropriate new Agency. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-02889287b04fa632877fa83f3f14e2de]

(5) Section 27 (obtaining of information by the Commission *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4] etc *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4]) shall have effect in relation to the appropriate new Agency, in its relevant capacity, as it has effect in relation to *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4] the Health and Safety Executive[^key-211e0c41a4693261acdc159fdc8a9708] *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4] (and not as it has effect in relation to an enforcing authority), except that the consent of the Secretary of State shall not be required to the service by the appropriate new Agency of a notice under subsection (1) of that section; and, accordingly, where that section has effect by virtue of this sub-paragraph— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4]

- (a (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4] any reference in that section to the Commission shall be construed as a reference to the appropriate new Agency; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4]
- (b (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4] any reference to an enforcing authority shall be disregarded; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4]
- (c (Extent: S) (affected by repeal, see note))[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4] in subsection (3) of that section, the words from “and also” onwards shall be disregarded. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-358514c1ec50fe670a33aa29f2b07bc4]

(6) In section 28 (restrictions on disclosure of information)—

- (a) in paragraph (a) of subsection (3) (exception for disclosure of information to certain bodies) after the words “the Executive,” there shall be inserted the words “ the Environment Agency, the Scottish Environment Protection Agency, ”;
- (b) in paragraph (c)(ii) of that subsection (exception for disclosure to officers of certain bodies) as it applies to England and Wales—
  - (i) the words “of the National Rivers Authority or”, and
  - (ii) the word “Authority,” (where next occurring),

  shall be omitted;
- (c) for paragraph (c)(ii) of that subsection as it applies to Scotland there shall be substituted—

  > - (ii) an officer of a water undertaker, sewerage undertaker, sewerage authority or water authority who is authorised by that authority to receive it;

  ;
- (d) paragraph (c)(iii) of that subsection (exception for disclosure to officers of river purification boards) shall cease to have effect;
- (e) in subsection (4) (references to certain bodies to include references to officers or inspectors), after the words “the Executive” (in the first place where they occur) there shall be inserted the words “ the Environment Agency, the Scottish Environment Protection Agency, ”;
- (f) in subsection (5) (information disclosed in pursuance of subsection (3) not to be used by recipient except for specified purposes)—
  - (i) in paragraph (a) (use for a purpose of the Executive etc) after the words “of the Executive or” there shall be inserted the words “ of the Environment Agency or of the Scottish Environment Protection Agency or ”;
  - (ii) in paragraph (b) as it applies to England and Wales (use for the purposes of certain bodies of information given to officers of those bodies), the words “**the National Rivers Authority**” shall be omitted;
  - (iii) in the said paragraph (b) as it applies to Scotland, for the words from the beginning to “in connection” there shall be substituted the words “ in the case of information given to an officer of a body which is a local authority, a water undertaker, a sewerage undertaker, a sewerage authority or a water authority the purposes of the body in connection ”.

(7) In section 38 (restriction on institution of proceedings in England and Wales) after the words “except by an inspector or” there shall be inserted the words “ the Environment Agency or ”.

(8) In this paragraph— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]

- “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**the appropriate new Agency *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**” means— *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]
  - (a) in relation to England and Wales, the Agency; and *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]
  - (b) in relation to Scotland, *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208] SEPA *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]
- “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**relevant capacity *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**”, in relation to the appropriate new Agency, means its capacity as the enforcing authority, for the purposes of Part I of the 1974 Act, which is responsible in relation to England and Wales or, as the case may be, Scotland for the enforcement of the relevant enactments; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]
- “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**the relevant enactments *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**” means the *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208] [^c14548811]Alkali, &c, Works Regulation Act 1906 and section 5 of the 1974 Act; *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]
- “ *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**the relevant statutory provisions *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]**” has the same meaning as in Part I of the 1974 Act. *(Extent: S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e9307fe058a93cad6d11b81db69a5208]

### The House of Commons Disqualification Act 1975 and the Northern Ireland Assembly Disqualification Act 1975

**31.** In Part II of Schedule 1 to the [^c14548821]House of Commons Disqualification Act 1975 (bodies of which all members are disqualified for membership of the House of Commons) the following entries shall be inserted at the appropriate places—

- (a) “The Environment Agency.”;
- (b) “The Scottish Environment Protection Agency.”;

and the like insertions shall be made in Part II of Schedule 1 to the [^c14548831]Northern Ireland Assembly Disqualification Act 1975 (bodies of which all members are disqualified for membership of the Northern Ireland Assembly).

**40.** In Schedule 4 to that Act (other powers etc. of licence holders) in paragraph 4(1)(b) (power for certain bodies to execute works involving alterations of electric lines or plant) for the words “National Rivers Authority” there shall be substituted the words “ Environment Agency ”.

**41.** In Schedule 5 to that Act (water rights) in paragraph 8(b) for the words “river purification authority within whose area the watercourse or loch affected is situated” there shall be substituted the words “ Scottish Environment Protection Agency ”.

**43.** In Schedule 5 to the Town and Country Planning Act 1990[^key-b6546104aefd09524bc41e05d0259c69] (conditions relating to mineral working) in paragraph 4 (consultations) after sub-paragraph (4) there shall be inserted—

> (4A) Without prejudice to the application of this paragraph in relation to consultation with the Forestry Commission, where the Minister is consulted pursuant to any provision of this paragraph—
>
> - (a) he is not required to inspect any land or to express a view on any matter or question; and
> - (b) he is not precluded from responding in general terms or otherwise in terms which are not specific to the land in question.

.

**44.** In Schedule 6 to that Act (determination of certain appeals by person appointed by the Secretary of State) in paragraph 1(1) (power, in respect of appeals under certain provisions, to prescribe classes of appeals to be determined by an appointed person instead of by the Secretary of State), after “208,” there shall be inserted “ and paragraphs 6(11) and (12) and 11(1) of Schedule 13 and paragraph 9(1) of Schedule 14 to the Environment Act 1995, ”.

**81.**[^key-f88b799d6878d7fb8fe89d3e473bef15] In section 63 of that Act (waste other than controlled waste) for subsection (2) (offences relating to the deposit of waste which is not controlled waste but which, if it were such waste, would be special waste) there shall be substituted—

> (2) A person who deposits, or knowingly causes or knowingly permits the deposit of, any waste—
>
> - (a) which is not controlled waste, but
> - (b) which, if it were controlled waste, would be special waste,
